Abstract: Altermagnetism is characterized by even-parity spin-momentum locking in spin-split bands despite zero net magnetization and negligible spin-orbit coupling. Here, we formulate a microscopic framework that links altermagnetic splitting in two-dimensional (2D) square lattices to orbital character. Using tight-binding models and symmetry analysis, we show that, within the minimal antiferromagnetic square-lattice model, single-orbital lattices remain spin-degenerate, whereas interwoven dual-orbital configurations lift Kramers degeneracy and generate d-wave or g-wave altermagnetic states. The spin-splitting originates from orbital anisotropy in the same-spin hopping channels. Guided by this framework, we identify M-TCNX (M = Cr, Mn, Fe; TCNX = TCNE, TCNQ) metal-organic framework monolayers with mcm topology as candidate g-wave altermagnets. Our work provides a symmetry-explicit wavefunction-level design framework for orbital-controlled altermagnetism in 2D square lattices.

</details>


### [95] [First-principles finite-size correction schemes for point defects of Cu$_3$N](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25153)
*Abdul M. Reyes,Sebastian E. Reyes-Lillo,Eduardo Menéndez-Proupin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过PBE+U结构弛豫与HSE混合泛函计算相结合，系统研究了Cu₃N中点缺陷的形成能和热力学转变能级。在256、864、2048原子超胞中量化了有限尺寸效应，扩展了Makov-Payne和Lany-Zunger修正方案，引入1/Lⁿ高阶项、芯级势对齐及缺陷特异性缩放模型。结果表明：强局域化带电空穴缺陷适用Makov-Payne型缩放，浅层/弱局域化间隙缺陷适用类氢杂质模型，中性或弱局域化缺陷需高阶多项式拟合。混合泛函计算确认Cu₃N在特定条件下为本征p型，且无单一修正方案可适用于所有缺陷类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确计算点缺陷的形成能和热力学转变能级依赖于可靠的有限尺寸修正，而现有修正方案在不同缺陷类型中的适用性尚不明确，尤其缺乏针对Cu₃N这一具有潜在光电应用价值的材料体系的系统研究。

Method: 采用PBE+U进行结构弛豫，结合HSE混合泛函计算缺陷形成能；使用256、864、2048原子超胞进行Γ点计算以量化有限尺寸效应；在Makov-Payne和Lany-Zunger修正方案基础上引入额外的1/Lⁿ高阶项、芯级势对齐和缺陷特异性缩放模型；针对不同局域化特征的缺陷采用不同的修正策略。

Result: 强局域化带电空穴缺陷适用Makov-Payne型缩放（1/L+1/L³）；浅层或弱局域化间隙缺陷适用类氢杂质模型；中性或弱局域化缺陷需1/L³和1/L⁴高阶多项式拟合。混合泛函修正证实Cu₃N为本征p型半导体，且无单一有限尺寸修正方案可普适所有缺陷类型。

Conclusion: 不同缺陷类型需要不同的有限尺寸修正策略，不存在普适的单一修正方案。基于PBE+U有限尺寸趋势校正的HSE混合泛函计算可有效确定Cu₃N的缺陷性质，确认其本征p型特征。

Abstract: Point defects play a key role in determining semiconductor properties, such as electrical conductivity and photoluminescence, and often enable functional behavior. Accurate first-principles supercell simulations of point defects require reliable finite-size corrections. In this study, we combine PBE+U structural relaxations with HSE hybrid-functional calculations to determine defect formation energies and thermodynamic transition levels of Cu$_3$N. Finite-size trends are quantified using $Γ$-point calculations in supercells containing 256, 864, and 2048 atoms. We assess and extend the Makov-Payne and Lany-Zunger correction schemes by introducing additional $1/L^n$ terms, together with core-level potential alignment and defect-specific scaling models. Using Cu$_3$N as a case study, we show that charged vacancies with strongly localized defect states are accurately described by the Makov-Payne-type scaling ($1/L + 1/L^{3}$), whereas interstitial defects with shallow or weakly localized electronic character are better captured by a hydrogenic impurity model that accounts for defect-band dispersion. Residual trends for neutral or weakly localized defects are described by higher-order polynomial fits in $1/L^{3}$ and $1/L^{4}$. Hybrid-functional energetics corrected using PBE+U-based finite-size trends confirm the intrinsic $p$-type character of Cu$_3$N under the conditions considered and demonstrate that no single finite-size correction can be transferred across all defect types.

</details>


### [96] [Exploring Multi-Transition-Metal NASICON Frameworks as High-Performance Cathodes for Sodium-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25219)
*Santosh Behara,Achinthya Krishna Bheemaguli,Gopalakrishnan Sai Gautam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过密度泛函理论计算，系统研究了包含Mn、Cr、Fe的九种NASICON型钠离子电池正极材料（涵盖一元、二元、三元组合），发现多元过渡金属体系表现出更复杂的相行为、Fe主导高电压、混合TM框架有利于Na⁺迁移（势垒0.3-0.4 eV），并识别出NaₓMnFe₀.₅Cr₀.₅(PO₄)₃为最有前景的三元组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找可持续、高性能的钠离子电池正极材料，系统研究多种过渡金属（Mn、Cr、Fe）在NASICON型磷酸盐中的作用，揭示组成复杂度与电化学性能之间的关系。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究九种包含Mn、Cr、Fe的NASICON组成（一元、二元、三元组合），分析其相行为、电压、电子结构和Na⁺迁移势垒。

Result: 一元体系在中间Na含量(x)处有明显稳定化；二元和三元体系表现出更复杂的相行为，部分体系热力学最小值从x=3移至x=2。Fe⁴⁺/Fe³⁺氧化还原主导高电压(~4.0 V)，Mn和Cr提供中低电压。混合TM框架有利于Na⁺迁移（势垒0.3-0.4 eV）。NaₓMnFe₀.₅Cr₀.₅(PO₄)₃在相稳定性、电压、热力学稳定性和迁移势垒方面表现最佳。

Conclusion: 揭示了NASICON中组成复杂度与热力学稳定性、电子结构、离子输运之间的相互作用，为利用多过渡金属NASICON作为高性能钠离子电池正极提供了可操作的设计原则。

Abstract: The search for sustainable, high-performance cathodes has driven a growing interest in sodium superionic conductor (NASICON)-type phosphates for sodium-ion batteries (SIBs). To identify promising NASICONs containing earth-abundant transition metals (TMs) and to systematically examine the role of multiple TMs in influencing the various properties of NASICON cathodes, we employ density functional theory calculations to investigate nine NASICON compositions containing Mn, Cr, and/or Fe, and spanning unary, binary, and ternary combinations. Our calculations reveal that unary systems, in terms of their Na intercalation phase behavior, exhibit well-defined stabilization at intermediate Na contents ($x$ in Na$_x$TM$_2$(PO$_4$)$_3$), while binary and ternary systems display more complex phase behavior, with some systems showing a shift of thermodynamic minima from $x$ = 3 to 2. Intercalation voltages highlight the dominant role of Fe$^{4+}$/Fe$^{3+}$ redox activity in elevating average voltages ($\sim$4.0 V), while Mn and Cr introduce intermediate-to-low voltage redox activity. Electronic structure data demonstrate non-systematic changes in the band gap, especially in systems containing multiple TMs. Na$^+$ mobility results identify mixed-TM frameworks as favorable, achieving Na$^+$ migration barriers in the 0.3-0.4 eV range. Importantly, we identify Na$_x$MnFe$_{0.5}$Cr$_{0.5}$(PO$_4$)$_3$ to be a promising ternary composition for subsequent experimental validation, offering an optimal intersection of phase stability, voltages, thermodynamic (meta)stability, and Na$^+$ migration barriers. Together, our study provides fundamental insights into the interplay between compositional complexity, thermodynamic stability, electronic structure, and ionic transport in NASICONs, and offers actionable design principles for utilising multi-TM NASICONs as high performance SIB cathodes.

</details>


### [97] [Composition-Driven High-Entropy Alloys with Enhanced Magnetocaloric Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25236)
*Nishant Tiwari,Juan Rafael Gomez Quispe,Noorbasha Bhavani Sai,Saikat Talapatra,Pedro Alves Da Silva Autreto,Varun Chaudhary,Chandra Sekhar Tiwary*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过实验与第一性原理自旋建模相结合，研究了由Fe、Ni、Co、Cr、Cu等地球丰富元素组成的单相立方高熵合金的磁热效应。发现等原子比和非等原子比合金均表现出连续的铁磁-顺磁转变，Cu含量会稀释磁亚点阵并降低居里温度，提供了一种通过成分调控磁热工作温度的定量设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金作为有前景的磁热材料，其工作温度可通过成分调控，但缺乏系统的定量设计准则。本文旨在通过实验与理论结合，建立成分-磁热性能关系，为过渡族金属高熵合金的磁热应用提供设计指南。

Method: 结合实验（DSC、磁测量）与第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）模拟，计算自旋极化电子结构；将交换耦合映射到经典海森堡模型，利用原子级蒙特卡洛模拟预测居里温度。通过控制理论Cu含量扫描，建立成分与TC的定量关系。

Result: 等原子比（Fe20Ni20Co20Cr20Cu20）合金TC≈110 K；非等原子比（Fe34Ni17.7Co24.8Cr15.2Cu8.3）合金TC≈420 K。在1.6 T磁场下，熵变1.24 J/(kg·K)，相对冷却功率92 J/kg。DFT表明减少Cu可增强Fe/Co/Ni的3d态自旋极化，强化铁磁交换。Cu含量增加会单调稀释磁亚点阵并降低TC。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通过调控Cu含量来定量调节过渡族高熵合金磁热工作温度的设计方法，为开发基于地球丰富元素的低成本磁热材料提供了理论指导。

Abstract: High entropy alloys (HEAs) are promising magnetocaloric materials with tunable operating temperature conditions using compositional modifications. Here, we combine experiments and first principles based spin modelling to engineer magnetocaloric response in single-phase cubic HEAs consisting of earth-abundant elements such as Fe, Ni, Co, Cr, and Cu. An equiatomic (Fe20Ni20Co20Cr20Cu20) and a Fe and Co rich non equiatomic (Fe34Ni17.7Co24.8Cr15.2Cu8.3) show a continuous ferromagnetic to paramagnetic transition with Curie temperature (TC)=110 K and TC=420 K for non equiatomic. Under the 1.6 Tesla magnetic field, the investigated alloy shows the entropy change =1.24 J per kgK with relative cooling power (RCP) =92 J per kg due to a broader effective cooling span. Density functional theory simulations reveal that reducing Cu enhances the spin-polarized Fe, Co, or Ni, 3d weight near fermi level, consistent with stronger ferromagnetic exchange. Exchange couplings mapped onto a classical Heisenberg model and solved by atomistic Monte Carlo to theoretically predict TC of both the investigated alloys. A controlled theoretical Cu sweep in equimolar further confirms that increasing Cu monotonically dilutes the magnetic sublattice and lowers TC, providing a quantitative design guideline to tune magnetocaloric operating temperatures in transition metal HEAs.

</details>


### [98] [Imaging Surface Magnetization in Altermagnetic MnTe Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25241)
*Ling-Jie Zhou,Senlei Li,Zi-Jie Yan,Yufei Zhao,Hongtao Rong,Zelong Xiong,Yiran Zhao,Pu Xiao,Lok Kan Lai,Hyeonhu Bae,Haoyu Liu,Chao-Xing Liu,Binghai Yan,Cui-Zu Chang,Hailong Wang,Chunhui Rita Du*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用扫描探针量子显微镜对原型交变磁体候选材料α-MnTe薄膜进行纳米级量子传感，可视化其弱磁化强度和磁畴结构，揭示了界面起源的弱磁化与反常霍尔效应的关联，为交变磁体集成自旋电子器件设计提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体具有显著的自旋分裂能带结构、非常规磁性和晶体对称性以及奇异磁输运性质，在自旋电子学前沿研究中备受关注。对交变磁体中自发磁畴和磁相的微观成像是探究其潜在材料性质、机制和自旋行为的重要步骤。

Method: 采用扫描探针量子显微镜对原型交变磁体候选材料α-MnTe的外延薄膜进行纳米级量子传感，可视化其渐逝磁化及相关磁畴，通过外加磁场控制固有交变磁序和构型，并评估不同厚度（下至原子尺度）的MnTe薄膜。

Result: 实现了对MnTe薄膜中弱磁化和相关磁畴的可视化；发现外加磁场可控制交变磁序和构型；观察到界面起源的弱磁化与其反常霍尔效应之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 研究结果推进了对新兴交变磁性的理解，为未来交变磁体集成自旋电子器件的材料设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Altermagnets with pronounced spin-splitting band structure, unconventional magnetic and crystal symmetries, and exotic magneto-transport properties have received immense interest in cutting-edge spintronics, materials science, and condensed matter physics research. Microscopic imaging of spontaneous magnetic domains and phases in altermagnets constitutes an important step for investigating their underlying material properties, mechanisms, and spin behaviors. Taking advantage of scanning-probe quantum microscopy, here we report nanoscale quantum sensing of a prototypical altermagnet candidate $α$-MnTe. We visualize evanescent magnetization and the associated magnetic domains in epitaxial MnTe films, which allows external magnetic fields to control the intrinsic altermagnetic order and configurations. By evaluating a series of MnTe films with different thicknesses down to the atomic scale, we further present evidence for the interfacial origin of the observed weak magnetization and show its correlation with the anomalous Hall effect in MnTe film. Our results advance the current understanding of emergent altermagnetism, providing insights into future material design of altermagnet-integrated spintronic devices.

</details>


### [99] [Thermal PBE in warm dense matter: Does it matter and is it accurate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25270)
*Kushal Ramakrishna,Mani Lokamani,Zhandos A. Moldabekov,Tobias Dornheim,Kieron Burke,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论框架下实现并系统基准测试了热PBE泛函在温稠密物质计算中的表现，与LDA、PBE及热LDA对比，热PBE在能量、力、压力和电子电荷密度等性质上显著改善，与路径积分蒙特卡洛参考数据高度吻合，且计算成本几乎不变，证明了热PBE作为温稠密区精确半局域泛函的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于条件概率密度泛函理论推导出PBE GGA交换关联自由能的温度依赖性后，需要系统验证该热PBE泛函在温稠密物质计算中的实际表现和准确性。

Method: 在KS-DFT框架下实现热PBE泛函，与LDA、PBE及热LDA进行系统对比，并通过与路径积分蒙特卡洛参考数据比较来评估其在温稠密物质性质（能量、力、压力、电子电荷密度）上的表现。

Result: 热PBE显著改善了温稠密物质性质的描述，特别是与PIMC参考数据高度一致，且计算成本几乎不增加。

Conclusion: 热PBE是温稠密区一种精确且实用的半局域泛函，能以几乎无额外计算成本的方式获得高精度结果。

Abstract: Conditional probability density functional theory has recently been used to derive the temperature dependence of the Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) generalized gradient approximation (GGA) for the exchange-correlation (XC) free energy. We implement and systematically benchmark thermal PBE within Kohn-Sham density functional theory calculations of warm dense matter. Comparisons with the local density approximation (LDA) and PBE functionals, as well as thermal LDA, show that thermal PBE significantly improves the description of warm dense matter properties, including energies, forces, pressures, and electronic charge densities. In particular, thermal PBE exhibits close agreement with path integral Monte Carlo (PIMC) reference data at negligible additional computational cost. This work demonstrates the practical utility of thermal PBE as an accurate semilocal functional for simulations in the warm dense regime.

</details>


### [100] [Anomalous Subsurface Vacancy Stabilization Dictated by Geometry-Electronic Decoupling on Metal Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25351)
*Yiming Tan,Pai Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过高通量DFT计算和机器学习力场，发现FCC（Ir, Pt, Au）和HCP（Be, Zn, Cd）密排金属表面存在反常热力学反转：次表面空位比表面空位更稳定。该异常源于两种晶系不同的"几何-电子解耦"机制。这一反常热力学层级使Pt(111)在HER和ORR中具备动态自修复能力，并解释了Au(100)表面重构的临界缺陷阈值（~8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统配位依赖的断键模型无法解释某些密排金属表面次表面空位比表面空位更稳定的反常现象，挑战了经典标量缺陷模型，亟需揭示其物理起源并探索其对催化性能的影响。

Method: 采用高通量密度泛函理论（DFT）计算结合机器学习力场，系统研究FCC（Ir, Pt, Au）和HCP（Be, Zn, Cd）密排金属表面的空位形成能，分析表面弛豫和电子局域化特征。

Result: 发现了FCC和HCP两类晶系中次表面空位更稳定的反常热力学反转现象，其物理起源在两种晶系中本质不同。对于FCC体系，表面弛豫与实空间电子局域化诱导出类共价面内键合，形成"几何-电子解耦"机制；Pt(111)利用此反常层级实现了动态自修复，Au(100)的表面重构临界缺陷阈值被成功解码为~8%。

Conclusion: 该工作挑战了经典配位依赖的断键模型，提出了"几何-电子解耦"的新范式，为工程化调控催化剂表面完整性提供了全新思路。

Abstract: Vacancy formation energetics fundamentally govern the structural integrity and catalytic behavior of metal surfaces. Contrary to conventional coordination-dependent broken-bond models, we identify an anomalous thermodynamic inversion on close-packed surfaces across Ir, Pt, Au with face-centered cubic (FCC) lattice and Be, Zn, Cd with hexagonal close-packed (HCP) lattice, where subsurface vacancies are intrinsically more stable than surface ones. Using high-throughput DFT calculations and machine learning force fields, we demonstrate that the physical origins of this anomaly are fundamentally decoupled between the two crystal systems. For the FCC trio, pronounced surface relaxation and a profound real-space electronic localization induce directional, covalent-like intralayer bonding, materializing a "geometry-electronic decoupling" mechanism. Crucially, this unconventional thermodynamic hierarchy enables a dynamic "self-healing" mechanism on Pt(111) that preserves an intact topmost layer and prevents catalytic degradation during hydrogen evolution and oxygen reduction reactions. It also successfully decoding the critical defect threshold (~8%) for lifting the Au(100) surface reconstruction. Our work challenges classical scalar defect models and provides a fresh paradigm for engineering catalyst surface integrity.

</details>


### [101] [Effects of Band Symmetry on Spin-Dependent Transport in Noncollinear Antiferromagnetic Tunnel Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25369)
*Mohamed Elekhtiar,Ding-Fu Shao,Evgeny Y. Tsymbal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究了Mn3NiN/LaAlO3/Mn3NiN反铁磁隧道结，发现能带对称性在自旋相关输运中起关键作用，并预测超过2000%的隧穿磁电阻（TMR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁隧道结（AFMTJ）具有大TMR，有望用于超快且抗磁场的自旋电子器件，但能带对称性对自旋依赖输运的影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论结合量子输运计算，研究基于Mn3NiN非共线Γ4g反铁磁相和LaAlO3势垒的(001)隧道结。

Result: 轨道对称性选择定则抑制平行构型的带间传输；反平行构型沿二维布里渊区对角线方向实现对称性兼容的带间隧穿，增强反平行电导，降低TMR（但仍超过2000%）。

Conclusion: 能带对称性过滤是AFMTJ中自旋依赖隧穿的关键因素，决定了TMR的可实现幅度。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic tunnel junctions (AFMTJs) can exhibit large tunneling magnetoresistance (TMR), making them promising candidates for ultrafast and field-robust spintronic devices. Here, we elucidate the role of band symmetry in governing spin-dependent transport in AFMTJs. Using first-principles density-functional theory combined with quantum-transport calculations, we investigate Mn3NiN/LaAlO3/Mn3NiN (001) junctions based on the noncollinear $Γ_{4g}$ antiferromagnetic phase of Mn3NiN. Although Mn3NiN exhibits a large momentum-dependent spin polarization due to broken $PT$ symmetry, we show that the tunneling conductance is critically controlled by band symmetry of the electrode Bloch states and their symmetry-selective coupling to evanescent states in the LaAlO3 barrier. Orbital-symmetry selection rules suppress interband transmission in the parallel configuration, whereas the antiparallel configuration enables symmetry-compatible interband tunneling along the diagonal directions of the two-dimensional Brillouin zone. These additional transmission channels enhance the antiparallel conductance and reduce the TMR relative to predictions based solely on spin polarization. Nevertheless, the TMR remains exceptionally large, exceeding 2000%, while band symmetry controls the attainable magnitude of TMR in AFMTJs. Our results establish band-symmetry filtering as an essential ingredient of spin-dependent tunneling in AFMTJs.

</details>


### [102] [Three-Dimensional Atomic-Scale Structural Transformation in a SrTiO3 Grain Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25410)
*Xiaoyue Gao,Jiake Wei,Bo Han,Junpin Luo,Ruilin Mao,Xiaowen Zhang,Xiaomei Li,Ryo Ishikawa,Bin Feng,Naoya Shibata,Yuichi Ikuhara,Peng Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用多片电子叠层衍射成像技术，首次在三维原子尺度上解析了SrTiO₃中Σ13(510)/[001]倾侧晶界的结构，发现了投影成像中隐藏的对称→非对称构型转变，并揭示了其与局域化学分布、空位分布、原子位移场及氧八面体旋转的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂氧化物中晶界的3D原子结构因传统(扫描)透射电镜的投影限制而难以解析，而晶界功能特性与其3D原子构型和局域化学环境密切相关，因此亟需深度分辨的表征方法。

Method: 采用多片电子叠层衍射成像（multislice electron ptychography）实现深度分辨成像，同时对阳离子和氧柱进行可视化，并结合原子柱强度定量分析、原子位移场映射和氧八面体旋转分析。

Result: 发现了投影成像中隐藏的对称STR1构型到非对称STR2构型的转变；两种构型具有不同的局域化学分布和空位分布；STR1→STR2转变涉及晶核局域原子重组和相邻晶粒的集体剪切位移；氧八面体旋转在STR2区域呈现显著不对称性。

Conclusion: 建立了3D原子结构、局域化学组成和晶格序参量之间的直接联系，强调了深度分辨表征在理解和工程化复杂氧化物晶界功能中的关键重要性。

Abstract: Grain boundaries (GBs) in complex oxides play critical roles in governing their functional properties, which are intrinsically linked to their three-dimensional (3D) atomic configurations and local chemical environments that can deviate markedly from those of the bulk. However, the 3D atomic structures of GBs remain poorly understood due to the projection limitations of conventional (S)TEM. Here, using multislice electron ptychography, we resolve the 3D atomic structure of a Σ13(510)/[001] tilt GB in SrTiO3 with simultaneous visualization of both cation and oxygen columns. Depth-resolved reconstruction reveals pronounced structural inhomogeneity along the GB, uncovering a transition from the canonical symmetric configuration (STR1) to an asymmetric configuration (STR2) that is hidden in conventional projection imaging. Quantitative analysis of atomic-column intensities demonstrates that these two GB configurations possess distinct local chemical and vacancy distributions. By further mapping the atomic displacement fields, we reveal that the transformation between STR1 and STR2 proceeds via local atomic shuffling at the GB core and collective shear displacement in the adjoining grains, mediated by the step and dislocation character of the junction, respectively. Moreover, analysis of oxygen octahedral rotations reveals a strong dependence on the local atomic structure with pronounced asymmetry around the STR2 region. These findings establish a direct link among the 3D atomic structure, local chemical composition, and lattice order parameters at the GB, underscoring the critical importance of depth-resolved characterization in understanding and engineering GB-mediated functionalities in complex oxides.

</details>


### [103] [Disentangling the contributions of individual cations to magnetic order in a spinel high entropy oxide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25432)
*Mario Ulises González-Rivas,Chun-Fu Chang,Martin Bluschke,Jessica Freese,Peter Bencok,Ronny Sutarto,Teak D. Boyko,Robert J. Green,George A. Sawatzky,Liu Hao Tjeng,Alannah M. Hallas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用元素特异性磁力测定（XMCD）研究两种铁磁尖晶石结构高熵氧化物中磁性转变的机制，发现不同阳离子的磁矩增长速率强烈依赖于其3d晶体场能级填充和可参与的交换路径，主导的A-B子晶格交换使某些离子快速磁化，而竞争相互作用导致另一些离子表现出缓慢转变，非磁性取代可缓解这种差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵氧化物尽管具有极端化学无序性，却能拥有长程有序的磁态，但不同化学组分如何共同促成这些磁态的出现仍知之甚少。

Method: 利用X射线磁性圆二色性（XMCD）实现元素特异性磁力测定，研究两种铁磁尖晶石结构HEO：(Cr,Mn,Fe,Co,Ni)$_3$O$_4$和(Cr,Mn,Fe,Co,Ni)$_{2.4}$Ga$_{0.6}$O$_4$。

Result: 所有化学物种的磁转变同时发生，但磁矩增长速率强烈依赖于阳离子种类。主导的A-B子晶格交换使四面体Fe$^{3+}$和八面体Ni$^{2+}$快速磁化，而竞争相互作用导致八面体Fe$^{3+}$和Cr$^{3+}$表现出缓慢转变。非磁性Ga取代可缓解这种差异。

Conclusion: 定制高熵氧化物尖晶石的磁性需要详细了解其位点选择性和交换路径。非磁性取代通过引入断裂的磁键连来缓解竞争相互作用带来的磁阻挫。

Abstract: High entropy oxides (HEOs) can possess long-range ordered magnetic states despite their extreme chemical disorder. Very little is known about how the different chemical constituents in HEOs contribute to the emergence of these magnetic states. In this work, we leverage element-specific magnetometry attained via x-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD) to understand how magnetic order is driven in two ferrimagnetic spinel-structured HEOs with compositions (Cr,Mn,Fe,Co,Ni)$_3$O$_4$ and (Cr,Mn,Fe,Co,Ni)$_{2.4}$Ga$_{0.6}$O$_4$. We find that while the magnetic transition is simultaneous for all chemical species, the rate at which their magnetic moments grow is strongly cation dependent. This behavior is explained by the varying $\textit{3d}$ crystal field level fillings of the magnetic cations, which in turn determine their ability to participate in the different magnetic exchange pathways available in the spinel structure. Dominant $A$-$B$ sublattice exchange enables some species to harden rapidly ($\textit{e.g.}$ tetrahedral Fe$^{3+}$ and octahedral Ni$^{2+}$) while others exhibit a sluggish transition due to frustration from competing interactions ($\textit{e.g.}$ octahedral Fe$^{3+}$ and Cr$^{3+}$). Non-magnetic substitution suppresses these differences, introducing broken magnetic linkages that relieve frustration. Tailoring the magnetism of HEO spinels therefore requires detailed knowledge of both their site selectivities and their exchange pathways.

</details>


### [104] [Local Structural Signatures of Shear Bands in Metallic Glasses via Electron Nanodiffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25433)
*Huyen T. Pham,Daniel East,Chunguang Tang,Matteo Baggioli,Alessio Zaccone,Timothy C. Petersen,Amelia C. Y. Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过逆向截面TEM制样和低温离子抛光技术，结合电子纳米衍射新参数，直接观测到金属玻璃剪切带下方塑性区中呈45°排列的纳米级条纹状应变局域化区域，证明了金属玻璃的塑性变形源于协同的纳米级结构转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃中由变形引起的结构变化难以用传统成像和衍射技术量化，且TEM制样过程中的离子轰击会引入难以检测的结构修饰，因此需要发展新方法以保留并探测金属玻璃变形的内在原子结构。

Method: 采用逆向截面TEM制备弯曲形成的剪切带薄片，结合低温离子抛光减少制样损伤；利用电子纳米衍射导出灵敏的新参数，局域探测金属玻璃表面剪切台阶下塑性区的纳米尺度结构，并映射局域中心对称性和应变。

Result: 发现塑性区中存在与施加应变呈45°取向的纳米级条纹状区域，这些区域具有高密度的局域原子结构转变：中心对称性降低、剪切应变和法向应变增大，表明应变已在此局域化。

Conclusion: 金属玻璃的塑性变形源于协同的纳米级结构转变，为这一长期未解问题提供了直接的实验证据。

Abstract: Structural changes in a glass due to deformation are subtle and difficult to quantify using conventional imaging and diffraction techniques. Additionally, transmission electron microscopy (TEM) sample preparation using energetic ions often causes structural modifications that are challenging to detect in disordered materials. By preparing inverted cross-sectional transmission electron microscopy lamellae of shear bands formed during bending, and employing cryogenic ion polishing to minimize preparation artefacts, we preserve the intrinsic atomic structure. Using sensitive, new parameters derived from electron nano-diffraction, we directly probe the local nano-scale structure in the plastic zone beneath surface shear steps in metallic glasses. Mapping of local centrosymmetry and strain reveals nanoscale, stripe-like regions oriented at 45 degree to the applied strain where strain has localized. These regions exhibit a high density of local atomic structures that have transformed to configurations with reduced centrosymmetry and increased magnitudes of shear and normal strain. Our results demonstrate that plastic deformation in metallic glasses arises from coordinated nanoscale structural transformations, providing direct experimental insight into a long-standing problem.

</details>


### [105] [Spin layer groups and their corepresentations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25484)
*Zeying Zhang,Gui-Bin Liu,Mu Tian,Run-Wu Zhang,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统分类了具有周期性平面的自旋层群，并解析推导了其不可约核心表示，为低维磁性材料中对称性保护的特性和新颖量子态研究建立了基础框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维自旋空间群直接应用于二维系统时，由于各向异性轴和维度约化而存在不足，亟需建立适用于二维磁性系统的自旋层群分类体系。

Method: 对不等价的自旋层群进行系统分类，并解析推导其不可约核心表示。

Result: 完成了自旋层群的不等价分类，并得到了其不可约核心表示的解析形式。

Conclusion: 该分类为研究低维磁性材料中对称性保护的特性和新型量子态提供了基础理论框架。

Abstract: Spin layer groups are the crystallographic symmetry groups with a periodic plane, and their symmetry operations are inherited from three-dimensional (3D) spin space groups. However, the direct application of 3D symmetry groups to two-dimensional systems is often inadequate due to anisotropic axes and dimensional reduction. In this work, we systematically classify inequivalent spin layer groups and analytically derive their irreducible corepresentations. This classification establishes a foundational framework for investigating symmetry-protected properties and novel quantum states in low-dimensional magnetic materials.

</details>


### [106] [Transition from Homogeneous to Domain-Wall-Mediated Polarization Switching in BaTiO3: A Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25485)
*Po-Yen Chen,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过机器学习势分子动力学模拟，发现BaTiO3中铁电畴翻转存在从均匀翻转到畴壁介导翻转的明确转变，该转变由尺寸依赖的极化涨落驱动，且翻转路径和滞回响应强烈依赖于超胞几何及应力与电场的相对取向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BaTiO3中极化翻转可通过根本不同的机制进行，但决定具体翻转路径的条件尚不清楚。

Method: 使用基于MACEField模型的机器学习势分子动力学，系统改变超胞尺寸进行模拟；采用Shannon熵分析极化涨落与畴壁成核的关联。

Result: 发现了从均匀翻转到畴壁介导翻转的明确转变，矫顽场增加超过50%；Shannon熵分析建立了局部构型无序与宏观翻转行为之间的定量联系；翻转路径和滞回响应强烈依赖于超胞几何及应力与电场方向的相对关系。

Conclusion: 均匀翻转和畴壁介导翻转是BaTiO3中不同的物理机制，原子尺度模拟必须考虑系统尺寸才能正确捕捉实际的翻转机制。

Abstract: Polarization switching in ferroelectric BaTiO3 can proceed through fundamentally different mechanisms - yet the conditions that determine which pathway is realized remain poorly understood. Using machine-learning potential-based molecular dynamics with the MACEField model, we systematically vary supercell size to reveal a clear transition from homogeneous polarization switching to domain-wall-mediated switching, accompanied by a coercive field increase of over 50%. Shannon entropy analysis demonstrates that this transition is driven by size-dependent polarization fluctuations that promote 180 degree domain-wall nucleation - establishing a direct, quantitative link between local configurational disorder and macroscopic switching behavior. Furthermore, the switching pathway and hysteresis response are shown to depend critically on supercell geometry and the relative orientation of applied stress and electric field. These findings reveal that homogeneous and domain-wall-mediated switching are distinct physical regimes in BaTiO3, and that atomistic simulations must account for system size to correctly capture the operative switching mechanism.

</details>


### [107] [Alignment-free ultra-broadband parametric frequency conversion in lead-halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25718)
*Abhishek Shiva Kumar,Dusan Lorenc,Ayan A. Zhumekenov,Osman M. Bakr,Zhanybek Alpichshev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了在厚单晶铅卤化物钙钛矿中实现超宽带四波混频，无需相位匹配、角度对准或色散优化，产生覆盖近红外到中红外连续范围的明亮相干辐射。时间分辨测量表明发射源于晶体表面附近（相位匹配约束松弛），而巨大的三阶非线性保证了高效定向转换。该工作将LHP定位为超宽带非线性光子学的强大体材料平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铅卤化物钙钛矿虽已展示已知最大光学非线性之一，但其在频率转换方面的潜力尚未被充分开发，现有技术需要复杂的相位匹配工程和对准优化。

Method: 在厚单晶LHP中实现近红外和中红外飞秒脉冲的超宽带四波混频，无需相位匹配工程、角度对准或色散优化；结合时间分辨测量定位发射源。

Result: 产生覆盖超宽连续调谐范围的明亮、相干、高度准直发射；发射源于晶体表面附近，此处相位匹配约束松弛；巨大的本征χ(3)响应保证了即使在局域化相互作用体积内也能实现高效定向频率转换。

Conclusion: 铅卤化物钙钛矿可作为超宽带非线性光子学的强大体材料平台，为紧凑、免对准的超快频率转换架构开辟新途径。

Abstract: Lead-halide perovskites were demonstrated to exhibit some of the largest known optical nonlinearities, yet their potential for frequency conversion remains largely untapped. Here we demonstrate ultra-broadband four-wave mixing of near- and mid-infrared femtosecond pulses in thick single-crystal LHPs, generating bright, coherent, and highly collimated emission across an exceptionally wide continuous tuning range without phase-matching engineering, angular alignment, or dispersion optimization. Time resolved measurements reveal that the emission originates near the crystal surfaces, where phase-matching constraints are relaxed, while the unusually large intrinsic $χ^{(3)}$ response preserves efficient and directional frequency conversion despite the strongly localized interaction volume. These results position LHPs as a powerful bulk platform for ultra-broadband nonlinear photonics, opening a pathway toward compact, alignment-free architectures for ultrafast frequency conversion.

</details>


### [108] [kikuchipy: an open-source toolbox for analysis of EBSD patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25722)
*Håkon W. Ånes,Phillip Crout,Lars Andreas Lervik,Ole Natlandsmyr,Tina Bergh,Jarle Hjelen,Antonius T. J. van Helvoort,Knut Marthinsen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: kikuchipy 是一个基于 Python 的开源 EBSD 分析工具箱，支持 Hough 和字典索引、取向/投影中心精化，可处理多厂商格式，并通过三个实际案例展示了其在相鉴别和取向分析中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为电子显微镜社区提供一个灵活、开源、可扩展的 EBSD 数据分析工具，摆脱商业软件的封闭性，促进算法改进和工作流程的快速迭代。

Method: 基于 Python 科学计算生态（如 NumPy/SciPy），实现 Hough 索引与字典索引双模式，支持主流厂商文件格式；通过独立于索引的映射和实验/模拟图案对比验证结果。

Result: 在三个案例中成功应用：超级双相不锈钢的取向关系分析、铸造改性 Al-Si 合金中 Al 与 Si 的相区分、Al-Mn 合金中 Al6Mn 与 alpha-AlMnSi 粒子的相鉴别。

Conclusion: kikuchipy 作为开源社区资源，任何人都可以改进或集成到自己的分析流程中，提升了 EBSD 数据分析的灵活性和可复现性。

Abstract: We present kikuchipy, an open-source toolbox for analysis of electron backscatter diffraction patterns, written in Python. The software is capable of both Hough and dictionary indexing and orientation and/or projection center refinement of patterns stored in file formats from all major vendors. Indexing results can be validated using maps independent of indexing and by visually comparing experimental and simulated patterns. By leveraging scientific packages in the Python ecosystem, emphasis is put on making the indexing workflow flexible and improve results through fast iteration. The software's capabilities are demonstrated on three application examples: analysis of orientation relationships in a super duplex stainless steel, phase differentiation of aluminium and silicon in a cast modified Al-Si alloy, and phase differentiation of particles in an Al-Mn alloy as Al6Mn or alpha-AlMnSi. The diffraction patterns and analysis workflows are made publicly available. kikuchipy was created and is developed as a resource for the electron microscopy community, allowing anyone to improve the software or include it into their own analysis workflows or softwares.

</details>


### [109] [Dealloying by peritectic melting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25834)
*Mingwang Zhong,Alain Karma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过相场模拟和锐界面理论，揭示了Ti-Ag包晶熔化中双连续结构源于液膜迁移的三维形态不稳定性，解释了实验观察到的组织演变和粗化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验已观察到Ti-Ag包晶熔化可形成双连续结构，但其形成机制尚不清楚，本文旨在阐明该机制。

Method: 采用相场模拟三维液膜迁移过程，并结合锐界面理论预测凝固前沿速度和初始韧带宽度。

Result: 发现双连续结构源于富Ti固相在富Ag液膜中生长时的分支状海藻/枝晶形态，侧枝合并形成手柄状拓扑结构；凝固速度及初始韧带宽度在时间上恒定，后续遵循t^{1/3}粗化规律。

Conclusion: 包晶熔化中的双连续结构由液膜迁移的形态不稳定性驱动，其演变机制与液态金属脱合金不同，粗化规律与实验结果吻合。

Abstract: Peritectic melting of Ti--Ag has been shown experimentally to form bicontinuous structures, but the mechanism remains unclear. Here we use phase-field simulations to show that these structures arise from a morphological instability of liquid film migration in three dimensions: a Ti-rich solid growing through an Ag-rich liquid film develops a branched seaweed or dendritic structure whose side branches coalesce to form handles, generating a high-genus bicontinuous topology. A sharp-interface theory predicts a solidification-front velocity and an initial ligament width that are constant in time, in contrast to liquid metal dealloying; subsequent $t^{1/3}$ coarsening reproduces the experimentally observed final ligament width.

</details>


### [110] [Benchmarking Transparent Conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25904)
*Amit Cohen,Lior Kornblum*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以应用所需薄层电阻(R_S)为固定约束来评估透明导电氧化物(TCOs)性能的基准框架，替代了传统与器件实际需求脱节的品质因数，从而为材料开发和选择提供更直接的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TCO材料设计依赖的品质因数与器件实际的电学需求脱节，导致材料开发条件在实际器件应用中不可行，需要建立与器件需求直接关联的评估方法。

Method: 提出以固定器件相关薄层电阻(R_S)为约束的基准测试框架，定义新指标T_app(R_S)——在给定R_S下可获得的光学透过率，据此评估和比较不同TCO材料的性能。

Result: 该框架能够定义各应用场景相关的薄层电阻需求图谱，并映射不同材料在其间的性能表现，提供可直接解读的绝对透过率增益度量。

Conclusion: 本文方法建立了一种在固定操作约束下评估材料的一般策略，弥合了材料设计与器件集成之间的鸿沟，为TCO应用导向的材料开发和选择提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Transparent conducting oxides (TCOs) are central to optoelectronic technologies, yet their design is often guided by popular figures of merit that are disconnected from the electrical requirement of actual devices. As a result, widely used metrics guide material design under conditions that can be impractical for devices. Here, we introduce a benchmarking framework to guide TCO development, in which transparent conductors are evaluated at fixed, application-relevant sheet resistance $(R_S)$. The resulting metric, $T_{app}(R_S)$, anchors comparison to device requirements, asking instead: What optical transparency can be obtained at the sheet resistance required by a given application? This approach provides a directly interpretable measure of performance, enabling materials to be benchmarked in terms of absolute transparency gains at a specified $R_S$. Applied to representative conventional and emerging TCOs, the framework defines the sheet-resistance landscape relevant to each application and maps how different materials perform within it. In doing so, it provides an application-rooted guide to material development and selection. More broadly, this approach establishes a general strategy for evaluating materials under fixed operational constraints, bridging the gap between materials design and device integration.

</details>


### [111] [Anomalous Hall Effect in Silicon-Compatible Altermagnetic alpha-MnTe Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25953)
*Rajib Sarkar,Subhransu Kumar Negi,Arindom Das,Arijit Mandal,Pankaj Bhardwaj,Sohini Guin,Aryaman Das,Naresh Shyaga,Laxmipriya Nanda,B. R. K. Nanda,Dhavala Suri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了通过分子束外延在Si(111)衬底上直接外延生长α-MnTe薄膜，并在这一硅兼容的交变磁性体系中实现了稳健的反常霍尔效应，为将Berry相驱动功能集成到可扩展半导体器件中提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自旋电子学的核心目标是将自旋依赖功能与主流半导体技术集成，但大多数候选材料与硅基平台不兼容，亟需寻找可直接在硅上生长的交变磁性材料体系。

Method: 采用分子束外延技术在Si(111)衬底上直接外延生长α-MnTe薄膜，结合高分辨率结构与光谱表征确认外延质量，通过磁输运测量研究霍尔响应，并利用第一性原理计算分析Berry曲率机制。

Result: 成功实现了相纯的六方NiAs型结构外延生长；薄膜表现出明显的磁滞霍尔响应（反常霍尔效应），且在横向和纵向输运通道中均存在关联磁滞行为，呈现系统性的温度演化规律；第一性原理计算证实了由自旋劈裂能带结构产生的未补偿Berry曲率是交变磁性对称性的体现。

Conclusion: MnTe/Si(111)是一种硅兼容的交变磁性平台，为将Berry相驱动功能嵌入可扩展的半导体器件架构提供了具体的技术路径。

Abstract: Integrating spin-dependent functionality with mainstream semiconductor technology is a central goal of modern spintronics, yet most candidate materials remain incompatible with silicon-based platforms. Here, we report the direct epitaxial integration of alpha-MnTe thin films on Si(111) via molecular beam epitaxy and demonstrate a robust anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in this silicon-compatible altermagnetic system. Despite the absence of net magnetization, the films exhibit a pronounced hysteretic Hall response, providing clear evidence of finite Berry curvature generated by symmetry breaking in the thin-film geometry. High resolution structural and spectroscopic characterization confirms phase-pure, epitaxial growth with hexagonal NiAs-type symmetry, while magnetotransport measurements reveal correlated hysteresis in both transverse and longitudinal channels with systematic temperature evolution. First-principles calculations reveal substantial uncompensated Berry curvature arising from the spin-split band structure consistent with altermagnetic symmetry and the origin of the observed Hall response. These results establish MnTe/Si(111) as a silicon-compatible altermagnetic platform and chart a concrete pathway for embedding Berry-phase-driven functionalities into scalable semiconductor device architectures.

</details>


### [112] [Light-Induced Transient Polarization Reversal in Rhombohedrally Stacked Bilayer Transition-Metal Dichalcogenides via an Electronic Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25982)
*Xiangzhou Zhu,Stefano Mocatti,Matteo Calandra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过约束密度泛函理论和多体实时模拟，发现在菱方堆叠的过渡金属二硫族化物双层中，光激发可在200飞秒内实现面外极化方向的超快电子反转，比传统剪切模式快约50倍，且无需层间滑动，为亚皮秒超快易失性光学存储器提供了新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光致滑动铁电性依赖层间剪切模式（数十皮秒）且需要极高光通量，易损伤样品，亟需更快速、低损伤的极化反转方案。

Method: 采用约束密度泛函理论（cDFT）和多体实时模拟，研究菱方堆叠过渡金属二硫族化物（TMD）双层在光激发下的电子结构和极化响应。

Result: 在中等光通量下，200飞秒内实现总面外极化符号反转，速度比典型剪切模式周期快约50倍；该反转由钨位点周围局域偶极子的重新排列驱动。

Conclusion: 建立了一种适用于所有具有II型能带排列的极性多层材料的电子控制低维铁电体新机制，对亚皮秒级超快易失性光存储有直接应用前景。

Abstract: Light-induced sliding ferroelectricity in two-dimensional van der Waals materials enables polarization control via relative layer motion. However, polarization switching occurs on the time scale of shear modes (tens of ps) and requires very large fluences, potentially damaging the samples. Here, using constrained density functional theory and many-body real-time simulations, we demonstrate an ultrafast electronic reversal of the total out-of-plane polarization sign in the photoexcited state, without requiring interlayer sliding, in rhombohedrally stacked transition-metal dichalcogenide bilayers. The polarization changes sign relative to its initial ground-state value at moderate fluences and within 200 fs, about 50 times faster than the typical shear-mode period. The ultrafast switching is driven by a rearrangement of localized dipoles around the tungsten sites. We establish a novel general mechanism for electronic control of low-dimensional ferroelectrics common to all polar multilayers having type II band alignment. Our work has direct implications for ultrahigh-speed volatile optical memory operating on sub-ps time scales.

</details>


### [113] [Nuclear-Electron Hyperfine Coupling of the Shallow States Associated with Vacancies in Gallium Nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26053)
*Joseph Sink,Michael E. Flatté*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用多带实空间格林函数和开放边界条件，精确求解了氮化镓中单个氮空位和镓空位的电子结构，并计算了局域态密度及超精细场的费米接触和各向异性贡献，这些量直接影响电检测磁共振信号，可用于识别GaN器件中的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究氮化镓（GaN）中单空位缺陷的电子结构和超精细相互作用，以便通过电检测磁共振信号识别这些缺陷。

Method: 采用多带实空间格林函数方法，在开放边界条件下求解纯净GaN的势散射Dyson方程，从而获得单个氮空位和镓空位的电子结构。

Result: 计算得到了局域态密度，以及缺陷附近超精细场的费米接触项和各向异性贡献。

Conclusion: 所计算的物理量直接影响电检测磁共振信号，可用于识别GaN器件中存在的氮空位和镓空位缺陷。

Abstract: We use multiband real space Green's functions computed using open-boundary conditions for clean GaN to exactly solve the potential-scattering Dyson equation to obtain the electronic structure of single nitrogen and gallium vacancies. From these vacancy solutions, we compute the local density of states as well as the Fermi contact and anisotropic contributions to the hyperfine field in the vicinity of the defect. These quantities directly affect electrically-detected magnetic resonance signals, which can be used to identify these defects when present in GaN devices.

</details>


### [114] [Theory of Electrically Detected Magnetic Resonance of Silicon Vacancy-Related Spin Pairs in Silicon Carbide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26071)
*David A. Fehr,Corey J. Cochrane,Stephen R. McMillan,Nicholas J. Harmon,Patrick M. Lenahan,Michael E. Flatté*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稳态Lindblad主方程的定量理论，用于模拟碳化硅中硅空位相关自旋对的电检测磁共振（EDMR），成功解释了室温测量结果，并预测了低温下的超精细结构可分辨性及双量子跃迁在磁场传感中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立定量理论框架以准确模拟和预测碳化硅中硅空位相关自旋对的EDMR行为，从而为缺陷自旋的相干时间、电输运速率及磁场传感应用提供理论指导。

Method: 采用稳态Lindblad主方程，考虑V1a和V2a型深能级硅空位（接近0/-电荷态转变能级）与不完整K中心（氮相关复合物）之间的超精细相互作用、自旋结构和朗德g因子，构建自旋对模型并进行模拟。

Result: 理论成功描述了近期室温下归因于V1a硅空位的测量结果，提取了合理的缺陷自旋相干时间和电输运速率参数；预测低温下可分辨浅能级超精细结构；预测V2a硅空位相关自旋对的EDMR谱，并提出可通过电学读出硅空位负电荷态的双光子双量子跃迁来增强磁场传感。

Conclusion: 该理论框架不仅解释了现有实验，还预测了低温超精细结构及双量子跃迁的电学读出能力，为基于碳化硅硅空位的高灵敏度磁场传感提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: We present a quantitative theory for simulating the electrically detected magnetic resonance (EDMR) of silicon vacancy-related spin pairs in silicon carbide using steady-state Lindblad master equations. In our theory, we consider V1a and V2a deep level silicon vacancies near the (0/-) charge state transition level in proximity to a previously identified nitrogen-related complex, the incomplete K-center, due to the hyperfine, spin structure, and Landé g factor of the shallow state. Our theory describes recent room temperature measurements attributed to V1a silicon vacancies, with reasonable extracted parameters for defect spin coherence times and electrical transport rates. At lower temperatures we predict that the shallow level hyperfine structure may be spectrally resolvable. Finally, we predict the EDMR spectrum of V2a silicon vacancy-related spin pairs and predict that two-photon, double quantum transitions of the silicon vacancy's negative charge state can be electrically read-out for enhanced magnetic field sensing.

</details>


### [115] [Magneto-optic phonon resonances in magnetic topological EuCd2As2 via helical Raman spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26073)
*Jin Ho Kang,Liangbo Liang,Ioannis Petrides,Kai-Chi Chang,Subhajit Roychowdhury,Chandra Shekhar,Claudia Felser,Prineha Narang,Chee Wei Wong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过低温圆偏振与线偏振拉曼光谱，结合DFT计算，研究了FM-EuCd2As2（Tc=26 K）中声子模式的自旋-声子耦合和磁圆二色性（12.5 meV峰DCP达60%），并与AFM相（TN=9 K）对比，揭示了拓扑与磁性的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究FM-EuCd2As2中自旋-声子耦合、磁各向异性及拓扑物态与磁性的相互作用，为利用Weyl体系在热电、声子器件及拓扑量子计算中的应用奠定基础。

Method: 使用低温（4.2 K）低频率圆偏振和线偏振拉曼光谱，辅以密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，测量FM-EuCd2As2（Tc=26 K）和AFM-EuCd2As2（TN=9 K）的声子模式，分析拉曼强度异常增强、磁光效应下的A模分辨及圆偏振度（DCP）。

Result: 居里温度以下拉曼强度异常增强归因于自旋-声子耦合；磁-螺旋拉曼光谱可通过磁光效应分辨A模峰；12.5 meV峰的DCP在4.2 K时达60%并饱和；AFM相中自旋-声子耦合微弱，DCP近乎为零。

Conclusion: 结果有助于理解FM-EuCd2As2中的声子动力学及拓扑与磁性的相互作用，为Weyl体系在热电、声子器件及拓扑量子计算中的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: EuCd2As2 materials have two magnetic ordering states: antiferromagnetic (AFM) and ferromagnetic (FM) when their chemical tunability is utilized. While AFM-EuCd2As2 has a nonzero magnetoelectric response due to its symmetry breaking with spin configuration, FM-EuCd2As2 is an ideal candidate for studies of Weyl physics because of its minimum number of Weyl points with opposite chirality. In this article, we examine cryogenic low-frequency Raman spectroscopy of phonon modes in FM-EuCd2As2 crystals using circular polarization configurations, with support from density functional theory calculations, and investigate in-plane magneto-anisotropy by linear polarization configuration below the Curie temperature (Tc = 26 K). We attribute the anomalous enhancements in Raman intensities below the Curie temperature are due to spin-phonon coupling. Furthermore, we see that A-mode peaks can be distinguished by magneto-helical Raman spectroscopy through the magneto-optic effect and that the degree of circular polarization (DCP) of 12.5 meV peak reaches 60% at 4.2 K and becomes saturated. We also examine AFM-EuCd2As2 below Néel temperature (TN = 9 K) to compare with FM-EuCd2As2, but we hardly observe spin-phonon coupling and find negligible DCP values due to almost zero net magnetization. Our results contribute to the understanding of the phonon dynamics and the interplay between topology and magnetism in FM-EuCd2As2, through helical light and external magnetic fields. This lays the foundation for utilizing state-of-the-art Weyl systems for applications in thermoelectrics, phononic devices, and topological quantum computing.

</details>
