### [116] [Mutual Friction in Dissipative Gross-Pitaevskii Thermal Counterflow Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25915)
*Kyo Yoshida,Hideaki Miura,Yoshiyuki Tsuji*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程研究热逆流湍流中的准稳态，发现相互摩擦加速度与超流-常流相对速度呈三次方标度关系，且系数与唯象阻尼参数相关；弱驱动下涡间距符合维度标度，涡线取向接近各向同性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解热逆流湍流中相互摩擦的标度律及涡旋动力学特性，弥补此前缺乏从第一性原理微观模拟定量验证的空白。

Method: 对耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程进行数值模拟，在多种强迫、阻尼和愈合长度参数下获得准稳态，并通过直涡线模型对比分析涡线取向。

Result: 相互摩擦加速度与相对速度呈三次方标度，系数与阻尼参数关联；弱驱动下涡间距符合预期维度标度；涡线取向接近各向同性。

Conclusion: 模拟结果首次定量验证了热逆流湍流中相互摩擦的三次方标度律，揭示了涡线取向的各向同性特征，为理解量子湍流现象提供了微观基础。

Abstract: We report numerical simulations of the dissipative Gross-Pitaevskii equation for a bulk region of thermal-counterflow turbulence. Quasistationary states are obtained over a range of forcing, damping, and healing-length parameters. The mutual-friction acceleration exhibits cubic scaling with the mean relative velocity between the superfluid and normal-fluid components, and the coefficient of this scaling is linked to the phenomenological damping parameter. The intervortex spacing follows the expected dimensional scaling in the weak-forcing regime. Comparison with a straight-vortex-line model suggests that the vortex-line orientations are nearly isotropic.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Supersymmetry Without Time-Reversal Invariance in Model A: A FRG perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24029)
*Sankarshan Sahu,Bertrand Delamotte,Adam Rançon,Matthieu Tissier*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明，在Model A动力学中，仅超对称性不足以确保系统弛豫到满足时间反演不变性（TRI）的稳态，需要额外条件。作者构建了超对称但违反TRI的模型，并论证在微扰下TRI仍作为有效大尺度对称性出现。利用泛函重整化群（FRG），证明动力学有效作用量包含平衡有效作用量的导数，且其重整化群流与平衡理论在导数展开中逐阶一致。最后，将该推理推广到伊辛模型总磁化概率分布的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清超对称性在Model A动力学中对于时间反演不变性（TRI）的充分性这一普遍误解，探究实现TRI所需满足的额外条件。

Method: 通过构建反例模型、微扰分析以及泛函重整化群（FRG）方法，从动力学有效作用量的结构出发，系统研究超对称性与TRI之间的关系。

Result: 超对称性并非TRI的充分条件；存在超对称但违反TRI的模型；在微扰下TRI作为有效大尺度对称性出现；动力学有效作用量中包含平衡有效作用量的导数项，且其RG流与平衡理论在导数展开中逐阶一致；该框架可推广至伊辛模型总磁化概率分布的恢复。

Conclusion: 超对称性需要额外条件才能保证TRI；但TRI在微扰下仍可作为大尺度有效对称性自然涌现；动力学与平衡理论之间存在深层结构关联，为从动力学恢复平衡性质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We show that, contrary to common belief, supersymmetry alone is not sufficient in Model A dynamics to ensure relaxation toward a stationary state satisfying time-reversal invariance (TRI). An additional condition on top of supersymmetry is required for TRI, which we analyze in detail. We explicitly construct a model that is supersymmetric but violates TRI, and argue that, at least perturbatively, TRI nevertheless emerges as an effective large-scale symmetry. Using the functional renormalization group (FRG), we further show that the dynamical effective action, $Γ[\varphi,\tilde\varphi]$, contains the derivative of the equilibrium effective action, $Γ^{\mathrm{eq}}[\varphi]$, whose renormalization-group flow is identical to that of the equilibrium theory order by order in the derivative expansion. Finally, extending the same line of reasoning, we show that the probability distribution of the total magnetization in the Ising model can be recovered within the Model A framework.

</details>


### [118] [Universal classification of continuous phase transitions with two order parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24061)
*Yilun Xu,Feng-xiao Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于Ginzburg-Landau范式，研究具有两个序参量的系统中的连续相变和临界现象，根据自由能中的耦合项对系统进行分类，揭示不同类别对应的相图模式和临界标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Ginzburg-Landau理论主要描述单序参量系统，其相图仅由正常相和有序相组成。但实际复杂系统往往涉及多个序参量，可能出现更丰富的相图和临界现象，目前缺乏系统的分类框架来理解这些行为。

Method: 基于自由能中的耦合项形式，将具有两个序参量的系统分为若干类别，分析每种类别对应的相图模式和临界标度行为。

Result: 不同类型的两序参量系统展现出不同的相图模式和临界标度规律，仅通过自由能表达式中耦合项的形式即可预测系统的相变行为。

Conclusion: 提出了仅基于自由能耦合项来研究和评估复杂系统的新途径，为多序参量系统的相变分析提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: The continuous phase transition, indicated by the macroscopic order parameter and the occurrence of the spontaneous symmetry breaking, is well illustrated based on the Ginzburg-Landau's paradigm. In systems described by one order parameter, the phase diagram is only composed of the normal phase and the ordered phase. However, in systems with two or more order parameters, much richer phase diagrams and critical phenomena may emerge. According to coupling terms of the free energy, we classify the systems with two order parameters into several categories, featuring different patterns of phase diagrams and critical scalings respectively. Our work propose the new avenue to studying and evaluating the complex systems only based on the coupling terms in expressions of free energy.

</details>


### [119] [Theory of supercooled water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24105)
*Jacobo Troncoso,Claudio A. Cerdeiriña*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种水在过冷液态（亚稳态）下的模型，数值解涵盖了从Speedy-Angell稳定性极限图像到Poole等人的亚稳态液-液临界现象。通过类比Joule-Thomson气体节流过程的“反转热力学”及其与黑洞现象的等价性，为液-液临界点的真实存在提供了重要的理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索水在深度过冷状态下亚稳态液-液临界点的真实存在性，并寻找新的理论依据。

Method: 建立水的过冷液态模型（数值求解），并通过类比Joule-Thomson气体节流过程的反转热力学及黑洞等价现象进行理论分析。

Result: 数值解成功复现了Speedy-Angell稳定性极限和Poole等人的液-液临界性；发现液-液临界点与Joule-Thomson反转热力学及黑洞现象存在未被注意的类比关系。

Conclusion: 液-液临界点的真实存在性因与Joule-Thomson过程及黑洞热力学的类比而获得了显著的理论可信度。

Abstract: We introduce a model of water contemplating true supercooled-liquid states that, as such, are metastable with respect to the crystalline-solid ones. Its numerical solutions reproduce from Speedy-Angell's stability-limit picture to Poole et al.'s metastable liquid--liquid criticality. The real existence of a liquid--liquid critical point is found to gain significant theoretical plausibility from a hitherto unnoticed analogy with the ``inversion thermodynamics'' of Joule-Thomson gas throttling process and, by extension, with the equivalent phenomenology exhibited by black holes.

</details>


### [120] [Resonant interactions in the $α$-FPUT lattice with site-dependent coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24268)
*Lorenzo Migliorelli,Giovanni Dematteis,Sergio Chibbaro,Miguel Onorato*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间变化系数对α-FPUT问题中波湍流动力学的影向，推导了新的动力学方程，表明热化速度显著加快，并包含促进波作用谱密度各向同化的Bragg散射项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将波湍流框架扩展到空间调制系数（弹簧刚度χ和非线性系数α随位置变化）的α-FPUT问题，探索系数空间变化对非线性能量传递和热化过程的影响。

Method: 采用波湍流理论框架，分析空间调制系数下三波相互作用的共振条件，推导新的动力学方程。

Result: 空间调制产生了非平凡的共振流形，推导出新的动力学方程，热化速度相比常数系数情况显著加快，方程中包含可关联至Bragg散射机制的额外项。

Conclusion: 系数空间变化能在波湍流系统中引入新的共振机制（如Bragg散射），显著加速热化过程并促进波作用谱密度函数的各向同性化。

Abstract: The wave turbulence framework has proven to be an effective tool for analyzing certain features of nonlinear energy transfer in one-dimensional nonlinear chains. In this work, we extend this approach to the $α$-FPUT problem when the spring stiffness $χ$ and the nonlinear coefficient $α$ are site-dependent. Although three-wave interactions are non-resonant for constant coefficients, their spatial modulation gives rise to a non-trivial resonant manifold. In this framework, we derive a new kinetic equation that suggests the possibility of substantially faster thermalization with respect to the constant coefficient case. The new kinetic equation includes also an extra term that can be associated to the Bragg-scattering mechanism, which promotes the isotropization of the wave-action spectral density function.

</details>


### [121] [Streaming Molecular Dynamics Simulation Data for On-the-fly Processing and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24271)
*Amruthesh Thirumalaiswamy,Lawson J. Woods,Heekun Cho,Hugo MacDermott-Opeskin,Jennifer Clark,Irfan Alibay,Yuxuan Zhuang,Oliver Beckstein,Matthias Heyden*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分子动力学模拟的流式传输框架，通过扩展IMD协议至IMDv3并集成到GROMACS、NAMD、LAMMPS中，配合Python包imdclient和MDAnalysis读取器，实现对模拟全量数据的实时访问与分析，避免了传统轨迹文件写入带来的数据丢失和存储瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MD模拟仅以10-100 ps间隔保存轨迹帧，丢弃了分子振动、溶剂动力学、短寿命过渡态和输运现象等快速过程的信息，而这些过程与宏观性质和实验可观测量直接相关。现有存储容量无法承受皮秒级全量数据（PB级），因此需要一种能够实时访问所有模拟数据的方案。

Method: 扩展Interactive Molecular Dynamics (IMD)协议至IMDv3；在GROMACS、NAMD、LAMMPS中实现IMDv3支持；开发Python包imdclient用于接收IMDv3数据流并暴露给外部应用；在MDAnalysis包中添加基于imdclient的读取器，使流式模拟数据可与传统轨迹文件一起分析。

Result: 基准测试表明，流式传输相比高频轨迹输出可提升性能。示例应用包括：自定义变量的实时监控、含快速波动的速度时间相关函数评估、膜孔电流的实时分析。

Conclusion: 该流式框架有效解决了MD模拟中数据存储瓶颈和快速过程信息丢失问题，为实时分析全量模拟数据提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Only a small fraction of the data generated in state-of-the-art all-atom multi-microsecond molecular dynamics (MD) simulations is typically analyzed. With femtosecond integration steps, microsecond simulations generate billions of time steps containing atomic positions, velocities, and forces, often corresponding to petabytes of data. Since this exceeds practical storage capacities, only a subset of frames is usually written to trajectory files at intervals of 10-100 ps. While sufficient for ensemble averages and slow dynamics, this approach discards information on fast processes such as molecular vibrations, solvent dynamics, short-lived transition states, and transport phenomena that are directly related to macroscopic properties and experimental observables. Here, we introduce a streaming framework for MD simulations that provides direct access to all data generated during a running simulation. Instead of writing high-frequency output to disk, the framework enables user-defined analysis and processing routines to access simulation data in real time. To achieve this, we extend the Interactive Molecular Dynamics (IMD) protocol and implement an enhanced version, termed IMDv3, in the MD packages GROMACS, NAMD, and LAMMPS. We further introduce the Python package imdclient, which receives IMDv3 data streams and exposes them to external applications. To maximize usability, we add an imdclient-based reader to the MDAnalysis package, enabling streamed simulation data to be analyzed alongside conventional trajectory files. Benchmark results show that streaming can improve performance compared to simulations with high-frequency trajectory output. Example applications include live monitoring of custom variables, evaluation of velocity time-correlation functions with fast fluctuations, and real-time analysis of membrane pore currents.

</details>


### [122] [Thermodynamics of classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24365)
*Yoshihiko Hasegawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从热力学角度推导了二元分类器中误差与热力学成本（熵产生和动力学活性）之间的基本权衡关系，证明了当热力学成本为零时，贝叶斯误差达到1/2（随机猜测），而更大的热力学成本可实现更低的误差，并讨论了该结果的量子推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似计算通过牺牲计算精度来降低能耗，这引出了一个根本性问题：信息处理中误差与热力学成本之间究竟存在怎样的关系？本文旨在从热力学第一性原理出发，建立这一基本权衡。

Method: 将分类问题建模为基于马尔可夫过程的信息处理，推导贝叶斯误差的下界与热力学成本（熵产生、动力学活性）之间的关系；并推广到量子情形，建立贝叶斯误差与哈密顿量方差的下界关系。

Result: 熵产生或动力学活性为零时，贝叶斯误差为1/2（等价于随机猜测）；热力学成本越大，可达的最低误差越低；量子分类器中贝叶斯误差受哈密顿量方差的下界约束。

Conclusion: 建立了信息处理中误差与热力学成本之间的基本权衡关系，为理解近似计算的热力学极限提供了理论依据，并揭示了热力学成本是超越随机猜测性能的必要条件。

Abstract: The Landauer principle bridges the energetic cost and information processing, showing that irreversible computation inevitably demands energy dissipation. As energy demands from computation continue to rise, approximate computing has attracted considerable attention. Approximate computing is based on the idea that energy consumption can be reduced by sacrificing computational accuracy. This raises a fundamental question about the relationship between error and thermodynamic cost in information processing. In this study, we derive the error-cost trade-off in the binary classifier by considering classification based on Markov processes. We obtain the lower bounds on the Bayes error in terms of thermodynamic costs such as entropy production and dynamical activity. Our results show that when entropy production or dynamical activity vanishes, the Bayes error reaches $1/2$, equivalent to random guessing, while greater thermodynamic costs enable lower error. This establishes a fundamental trade-off between error and cost in information processing by thermodynamic systems. Because the Bayes error provides the lowest achievable error among all possible classifiers, the classification error cannot fall below the obtained bounds given the entropy production or dynamical activity. We also discuss the quantum generalization and show that the Bayes error of the quantum classifier is bounded from below by the variance of the Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [123] [The peculiar response of Kelvin-Voigt chains with a free end](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24467)
*Rupayan Saha,Matthias Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了一个具有动量守恒耗散的过阻尼异构粒子链模型，通过前向差分变换实现非对称漂移算子的解析对角化，揭示了响应矩阵的阶梯结构，并展示了状态空间分解为自由子空间和约束子空间的物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究异构链中过阻尼粒子在动量守恒耗散下的精确求解问题，填补非对称漂移算子可解析对角化模型的空白。

Method: 通过前向差分变换对非对称漂移算子进行解析对角化；分析一端自由时的响应矩阵阶梯形式；对秩亏交互矩阵进行状态空间分解（自由子空间和约束子空间）。

Result: 1) 响应矩阵呈现阶梯结构：粒子i对粒子j所受力的响应与i、j之间的链段性质和长度无关；2) 自由子空间控制稳态响应，约束子空间控制外力停止后的弛豫过程。

Conclusion: 建立了分析具有动量守恒的异构过阻尼动力学的框架，明确了状态空间分解的物理意义。

Abstract: We exactly solve a model of a heterogeneous chain of overdamped, harmonically coupled particles with momentum-conserving dissipation. Despite being governed by a non-symmetric drift operator, the system admits an analytical diagonalization by use of a forward-difference transformation. In case of one free end, the response matrix shows a peculiar staircase form: the response of particle i to a force acting on particle j is independent of the properties and the length of the chain-part between i and j. For rank-deficient interaction matrices, the state space is decomposed into free and constrained subspaces. We demonstrate that this separation has clear physical consequences: the free subspace governs steady state responses, while the constraint subspace governs the relaxation after cessation of forcing. These results establish a framework for analyzing heterogeneous overdamped dynamics with momentum conservation.

</details>


### [124] [Implicit Binarization via Complex Phase Dynamics in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24502)
*Khen Cohen,Mark Glass,Meir Feder,Yaron Oz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受物理学启发的连续松弛框架，将离散二元变量参数化为复单位圆上的连续波状态，从而平滑NP-hard组合优化的非凸能量景观。该方法揭示了一种隐式正则化机制并显式应用，在160x160 QUBO任务中实现零误差，在欠定稀疏编码中优于OMP和LASSO，并在11个植解Ising基准中恢复8个精确基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NP-hard组合优化问题（如QUBO、稀疏编码和Ising模型）面临高度非凸的能量景观，传统离散优化方法容易陷入局部最优，且现有连续松弛方法缺乏有效的隐式正则化机制来引导收敛至离散状态。

Method: 将离散二元变量参数化为复单位圆上的连续波状态（复相位表示），平滑非凸能量景观；提取隐式正则化机制并将其显式应用于标准实值优化框架中。

Result: 在160x160 QUBO任务中（噪声sigma=0.25）实现零误差；在欠定稀疏编码中（sigma=0.15）实现完美恢复，优于OMP和LASSO；在11个植解Ising基准中恢复8个精确基态配置。

Conclusion: 基于复相位参数的连续松弛结合隐式正则化机制，能显著提升NP-hard组合优化问题的求解质量与鲁棒性，且该正则化机制可迁移至标准实值优化框架中。

Abstract: We introduce a physics-inspired continuous relaxation framework that yields substantially improved solutions for NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems, including Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO), binary sparse coding, and planted-solution Ising models. By parameterizing discrete binary variables as continuous wave-like states on the complex unit circle, we inherently smooth highly non-convex energy landscapes. We show that representing binary variables as complex phases reveals an implicit regularization mechanism that promotes convergence toward discrete states. Extracting this mechanism yields significant improvements even within standard real-valued optimization frameworks, using this regularizer explicitly. Empirically, this regularization yields vastly higher ground-state convergence rates than standard real-valued alternatives. Our models achieved zero error in large-scale 160x160 QUBO tasks under severe noise (sigma=0.25), and outperformed traditional algorithms (OMP and LASSO) in underdefined sparse coding with perfect recovery at sigma=0.15. The solver's robustness was further validated by recovering exact ground-state configurations in 8 out of 11 rigorously engineered planted-solution benchmarks.

</details>


### [125] [Long-range correlations in a locally constrained exclusion process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12632)
*Stefan Großkinsky,Gunter Schütz,Ali Zahra*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种具有简单局部动力学约束的排斥过程，展示了从短程相关均匀相到长程相关、自发破缺平移不变性的团簇相之间的显著转变。团簇的亚稳态动力学导致粗化级联和玻璃态动力学，并产生了一种有趣的“越快越慢”效应：流动方向上的不对称性增加反而导致稳态电流减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有简单局部动力学约束的排斥过程中可能出现的复杂相变和动力学现象，特别是长程相关性和自发对称性破缺的涌现机制。

Method: 引入一种新型排斥过程，该过程具有简单的局部动力学约束，通过分析粒子团簇的亚稳态动力学来研究系统行为。

Result: 发现了从均匀相到团簇相的显著转变；观察到粗化级联和玻璃态动力学；发现“越快越慢”效应，即流动不对称性增加反而降低稳态电流。

Conclusion: 该排斥过程展示了丰富的非平衡相变和动力学现象，特别是“越快越慢”效应为理解复杂系统中的反直觉行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce a novel exclusion process with a simple local kinetic constraint that leads to a remarkable transition between a homogeneous phase with short-range correlations and a clustered phase with long-range correlations and spontaneous breaking of translation invariance. The metastable dynamics of particle clusters lead to a coarsening cascade and glassy dynamics, as well as an intriguing faster-is-slower effect where an increasing asymmetry in the flow direction leads to a decrease of the stationary current.

</details>


### [126] [Strong Eigenstate Thermalization from Mean-Ergodic Non-chaotic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24510)
*Avadhut V. Purohit,Harshit Sharma,Udaysinh T. Bhosale*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从双 kicked 顶（DKT）模型出发，发现一个非混沌但均值遍历的多体系统在量子区域中表现出强本征态热化假设（ETH），证明强 ETH 并非混沌系统独有，也可出现在均值遍历系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一个关键开放问题：强 ETH 是否是遍历性（或均值遍历性）的量子类比？即强 ETH 是否必然要求系统具有混沌动力学？

Method: 基于双 kicked 顶（DKT）模型构造一个非混沌但均值遍历的多体系统，通过数值和理论分析，研究可观测量的对角矩阵元涨落（标度为 D^{-1/2}）以及非对角矩阵元的统计特性（高斯分布与平滑函数 f_O(Ē, ω)）。

Result: 非混沌的均值遍历系统仍满足强 ETH：对角元涨落按希尔伯特空间维度的平方根倒数标度 D^{-1/2} 衰减；非对角元呈现高斯统计，且其平滑函数 f_O(Ē, ω) 在大 k_θ 区域趋于均匀。

Conclusion: 强 ETH 并不局限于混沌系统，即使是非混沌的均值遍历系统也能展现强 ETH，表明强 ETH 可能是均值遍历性的一个量子体现。

Abstract: We report an example of a many-body system, derived from the double kicked top (DKT), with non-chaotic yet mean-ergodic dynamics that displays \textit{strong} eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) in the quantum regime. The analysis addresses a key open question: whether \textit{strong} ETH is a quantum analog of ergodicity (or mean-ergodicity). Despite non-chaotic dynamics, the fluctuations of the diagonal matrix elements of an observable scale as $D^{-1/2}$, where $D$ denotes the Hilbert space dimension. Furthermore, the off-diagonal matrix elements show Gaussian statistics together with a smooth function $f_O(\bar{E}, ω)$ that becomes nearly uniform in the large-$k_θ$ domain. Our findings show that even mean-ergodic and non-chaotic systems can exhibit \textit{strong} ETH.

</details>


### [127] [Rare events of small-noise Doob conditioned processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24537)
*Iago N. Mamede,Francesco Coghi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫过程Doob固定时间条件化的弱噪声大偏差统计，通过将条件化系综重新解释为原始过程在终端约束下的后选择，避免了显式构造Doob变换，并得到了生成函数指数级贡献的最优控制变分表示，在生物分子折叠的最小模型热耗散中验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Doob固定时间条件化能够通过对漂移项进行修改来采样马尔可夫过程的罕见轨迹，保证在给定时间到达指定目标，但Doob漂移通常难以获得闭式解，因此需要一种避免显式构造Doob变换的替代统计分析方法。

Method: 将条件化系综重新解释为原始过程在终端约束下的后选择（post-selection），从而避免显式构造Doob变换；在此基础上，推导出生成函数指数级贡献的最优控制表示，以带有Doob端点约束的变分原理形式表达。

Result: 得到了弱噪声大偏差下生成函数指数级贡献的最优控制变分表示，并通过两个解析示例和生物分子折叠最小模型的热耗散应用验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架为Doob条件化系综的统计研究提供了一种无需显式构造Doob变换的有效方法，在复杂系统的罕见轨迹采样和分析中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Doob fixed-time conditioning enables the sampling of rare trajectories of Markov processes by modifying the drift so that reaching a prescribed target at a given time is guaranteed. We study the statistics of this conditioned path ensemble through the moment generating function in the weak-noise large deviation regime. Since the Doob drift is rarely available in closed form, we reinterpret the conditioned ensemble as the original process post-selected on the terminal constraint, thereby avoiding explicit construction of the Doob transform. This viewpoint then yields an optimal-control representation for the leading exponential contribution to the generating function, expressed as a variational principle with terminal boundary conditions set by the Doob end-point constraint. We illustrate the framework with two analytical examples and with an application to heat dissipation of a minimal model of biomolecular folding.

</details>


### [128] [Manipulation of information flow and thermodynamic performance in nonreciprocal quantum dot information engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24554)
*Hao Feng,Junjie Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究基于双量子点结构的信息引擎，通过引入非互易耦合来调控工作物质与控制器之间的信息流，从而优化引擎性能。研究发现非互易性可等效映射为有效互易系统，并提供超越传统互易配置的控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息引擎中工作物质与控制器之间的信息流调控策略尚不明确，亟需探索有效的操控机制以优化能量转换效率。

Method: 将热力学第二定律扩展至包含非互易耦合效应，建立热力学框架；通过映射到有效互易系统（重参数化化学势和电子-电子耦合强度）进行等价理解。

Result: 非互易性可显著调制量子点间信息流，提供稳健的控制机制；非互易耦合可作为超越传统互易配置的有效控制手段。

Conclusion: 非互易耦合是设计和优化量子点信息引擎的有效控制旋钮，能超越传统互易配置的性能限制。

Abstract: Quantum information engines leverage information as a thermodynamic resource to facilitate energy conversion. In the operation of such engines, the information flow between the working substance and the controller is pivotal, however, strategies for its efficient manipulation remain largely unexplored. Here, we investigate an autonomous information engine based on a double-quantum-dot setup, where a downstream dot coupled to two reservoirs acts as the working substance, and an upstream dot coupled to a single reservoir serves as the controller. By extending the second law of thermodynamics to incorporate the effects of nonreciprocal couplings between the dots and their electronic reservoirs, we develop a thermodynamic framework that allows us to demonstrate that nonreciprocity can significantly modulate the inter-dot information flow, thereby providing a robust control mechanism. We show that the influence of nonreciprocity can be equivalently understood through a mapping to an effective reciprocal system upon a reparameterization of chemical potentials and the electron-electron coupling strength. We further analyze the impact of nonreciprocity on the engine's performance and operation regime. Our findings establish nonreciprocal coupling as an effective control knob for designing and optimizing quantum dot information engines, surpassing the capabilities of conventional reciprocal configurations.

</details>


### [129] [A particle-resolved rheological study of chirality transfer and odd transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25136)
*Rémi Goerlich,Alexander P. Antonov,Kristian Stølevik Olsen,Lorenzo Caprini,Christian Scholz,Hartmut Löwen,Yael Roichman*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过实验、模拟和理论相结合的方法，研究了嵌入在手性活性浴中的对称示踪粒子如何通过局部碰撞获得手性动力学，产生圆形轨迹和奇数输运（横向漂移），并揭示了非线性摩擦是将手性活性涨落整流为宏观奇数响应的关键微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解手性环境如何影响嵌入其中的示踪粒子，以及异常响应（如奇数输运）产生的微观机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用桌面实验、多体模拟和简化粗粒度理论相结合的方法，在非平衡手性活性浴中驱动对称被动示踪粒子，研究其流变学行为。

Result: 示踪粒子通过局部碰撞从手性活性粒子那里获得手性动力学，表现出圆形轨迹；在恒定拉力下产生系统性横向漂移（奇数响应）；非线性摩擦是整流手性活性涨落为宏观奇数输运的关键因素。

Conclusion: 揭示了手性活性物质中奇数输运的微观机制，为驱动非平衡系统中的横向输运提供了普遍见解。

Abstract: Chirality, or the breaking of mirror symmetry, appears across all scales in nature, from molecular conformations to the dynamics of bacterial collectives. Environments composed of such symmetry-breaking constituents can give rise to emergent physical phenomena, particularly in the transport and response of embedded tracers. Yet it remains unclear how chiral environments influence such tracers and through which microscopic mechanisms anomalous responses emerge. Here, we present a particle-resolved study of these systems, demonstrating chirality transfer and odd transport of an object embedded in a chiral active bath. In a rheological experiment, a symmetric passive tracer is driven through collisions with the particles of a non-equilibrium chiral bath. Combining table-top experiments, many-body simulations, and a reduced coarse-grained theory, we demonstrate that local collisions transfer chiral active dynamics to the tracer, which displays circular trajectories. We show that the same mechanism gives rise to a systematic transverse drift under a constant pulling force. Crucially, we identify nonlinear friction as an essential factor that rectifies these transferred chiral active fluctuations into a macroscopic odd response. Our results reveal a microscopic mechanism for odd transport in chiral active matter and provide general insights into transverse transport in driven non-equilibrium systems.

</details>


### [130] [First-passage time distribution of a Brownian particle harmonically confined in a viscoelastic bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25215)
*Brandon R. Ferrer,Juan Ruben Gomez-Solano*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实验上研究了谐振势阱中布朗粒子的首次通过时间特性，推导了过阻尼广义朗之万方程下首次通过时间概率密度的一般表达式，并在粘弹性聚合物、胶束溶液及粘性流体等不同介质中通过光镊实验验证了理论结果，发现粘弹性介质中的平均首次通过时间可超过相同零剪切粘度的粘性介质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非马尔可夫（粘弹性）环境中谐振势阱中布朗粒子的首次通过时间统计特性，填补现有理论在非马尔可夫情况下的空白。

Method: 基于过阻尼广义朗之万方程推导首次通过时间概率密度函数的一般表达式；利用光镊捕获胶体球，在粘弹性聚合物溶液、胶束溶液、甘油/水混合物及纯水等不同介质中进行实验测量，与理论进行定量对比。

Result: 推导出首次通过时间分布可隐式表达为摩擦记忆核的函数，并正确还原了马尔可夫粘性浴的已知结果；实验数据与理论定量吻合；发现粘弹性浴中平均首次通过时间可超过相同零剪切粘度的粘性介质，归因于前者首次通过时间概率密度出现缓慢衰减的尾部。

Conclusion: 建立了非马尔可夫环境中谐振势阱布朗粒子首次通过时间的理论框架，并通过实验验证其有效性，揭示粘弹性记忆效应对首次通过时间统计的显著影响。

Abstract: We investigate theoretically and experimentally the first passage-time properties of a spherical Brownian particle that is harmonically trapped at thermal equilibrium in a fluid at constant temperature. By using the overdamped version of the generalized Langevin equation, we derive a general expression for the probability density function of the time that the particle takes to reach for the first time the minimum of the potential starting from an arbitrary position. We show that such a first-passage time distribution can be implicitly expressed in terms of the friction memory kernel that encodes the interaction of the particle with its surroundings, and correctly reduces to previously found expressions in the case of a Markovian viscous bath. We validate our theoretical results by measuring the first-passage time of colloidal beads optically trapped in non-Markovian baths such as viscoelastic polymer and micellar solutions, as well as in a viscous glycerol/water mixture and water, which behave as Markovian media, having quantitative agreement with the derived expressions. In particular, we find that the mean first-passage time in a viscoelastic bath can surpass that in a viscous medium of the same zero-shear viscosity due to the emergence of slowly decaying tails in the first-passage time probability density of the former.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Local Detailed Balance: Microscopic Rates Reshape Nonequilibrium Phase Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25406)
*Takahiro Kanazawa,Kyogo Kawaguchi,Kyosuke Adachi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现，在满足相同局部详细平衡（LDB）条件下，不同的跃迁速率函数形式可以根本性地改变强相互作用非平衡系统的相行为，包括反转结构因子不连续性符号和切换各向异性相分离图案的取向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDB是建模非平衡随机动力学的核心原则，但它仅约束正反向跃迁速率的比值，不固定稳态。已有研究表明在弱相互作用系统中LDB相同但速率函数形式会影响关联性质，但在强相互作用系统中是否会影响相行为尚不清楚。

Method: 对具有吸引性最近邻相互作用的二维驱动格点气体，考虑一个保持相同LDB但沿驱动力方向调节跃迁不对称性的参数，通过分析均匀相的结构因子和各向异性相分离图案来研究相行为。

Result: 该参数能控制定性相行为：在均匀相中反转结构因子不连续性符号及长程密度关联的各向异性；在相分离区域切换各向异性图案的取向及其长时间稳定性。这两种效应都能被近似的涨落流体动力学方程一致地捕捉。

Conclusion: 与平衡系统不同，非平衡系统的相行为依赖于具体的动力学规则，即使这些规则遵循相同的LDB。

Abstract: Local detailed balance (LDB) is a central guiding principle for modeling nonequilibrium stochastic dynamics, yet it only constrains the ratio of forward and backward transition rates and does not fix the steady state. Although the functional form of rates under the same LDB has been shown to affect correlation properties in weakly interacting systems, whether it can reshape phase behavior in strongly interacting systems remains unclear. Here, for a two-dimensional driven lattice gas with attractive nearest-neighbor interactions, we consider hopping rates with a parameter that preserves the same LDB but tunes asymmetry along the driving force. We find that this parameter controls qualitative phase behavior: in the homogeneous phase, it reverses the sign of the structure-factor discontinuity and hence the anisotropy in long-range density correlations; in the phase-separated regime, it switches the orientation of anisotropic patterns and their long-time stability. Both effects are coherently captured by an approximate fluctuating hydrodynamic equation. The results demonstrate that, in contrast to equilibrium systems, nonequilibrium phase behavior depends on specific dynamical rules even when following the same LDB.

</details>


### [132] [Equivalent-neighbor $k$-core percolation in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25812)
*Qiyuan Shi,Ming Li,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模数值模拟，研究了二维空间中具有扩展相互作用范围r的k-核渗流的临界行为。结果表明，二维k-核渗流不存在混合相变，仅包含连续相变区和严格一级相变区，两者由三临界或临界端点分隔。在一级相变区，伪临界点以1/ln L的尺度漂移，而非传统的L^{-d}标度，表明该相变由成核机制驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限维度（二维）下，扩展相互作用范围r对k-核渗流临界行为的影响，特别是与无限维结果进行对比，揭示几何约束如何改变相变性质。

Method: 大规模数值模拟，系统变化核心指数k和相互作用范围r，构建(k,r)平面上的完整相图，分析有限尺寸标度行为。

Result: 二维k-核渗流无混合相变；相图由连续相变区（k<k_s, r<r_s，属于标准2D渗流普适类）和一级相变区（k>k_s, r>r_s）组成，两者由三临界/临界端点分隔；一级相变区伪临界点以1/ln L漂移，不同于传统热力学一级相变的L^{-d}标度。

Conclusion: 几何约束（有限维度）从根本上改变了k-核渗流的相变性质，其一阶相变由稀有空穴触发的成核机制主导，表现为对数形式的有限尺寸漂移。

Abstract: We perform large-scale numerical simulations to investigate the critical behavior of $k$-core percolation in two dimensions with an extended interaction range $r$. By systematically varying both the core index $k$ and the interaction range $r$, we construct a comprehensive phase diagram in the $(k,r)$ plane. In contrast to $k$-core percolation in infinite dimensions, no hybrid transition is observed in two dimensions: the phase diagram contains only a continuous transition regime and a strictly first-order regime, separated by a tricritical or critical-end point $(k_s,r_s)$. For $k<k_s$ and $r<r_s$, the transition is continuous and belongs to the universality class of standard two-dimensional (2D) percolation. For $k>k_s$ and finite $r>r_s$, the transition is discontinuous, with no hybrid features or critical singularities. In this first-order regime, the pseudocritical point approaches the critical point as $1/\ln L$, where $L$ is the linear system size, distinct from the $L^{-d}$ scaling typical of conventional thermodynamic first-order transitions in $d$ dimensions. This logarithmic finite-size drift is consistent with a nucleation-driven mechanism, in which rare voids trigger the collapse of the finite-range $k$-core. These results demonstrate that geometric constraints can fundamentally alter the nature of $k$-core percolation found in finite dimensions.

</details>


### [133] [Effect of slow bonds on current fluctuations in the symmetric simple exclusion process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26069)
*Soumyabrata Saha,Sandeep Jangid,Kapil Sharma,Tridib Sadhu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在局部慢键时对称简单排斥过程（SSEP）中粒子流的大偏差函数，针对三种几何构型给出了精确表达式，并通过罕见事件模拟验证，同时为半无限SSEP的流大偏差函数提供了初等推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非平衡动力学模型SSEP中粒子流的大偏差结果已通过可积性方法获得，但慢键（局部缺陷）如何修改这些结果尚待研究。本文旨在探讨慢键对三种几何构型下大偏差函数的精确影响。

Method: 针对三种几何构型：（a）有限一维晶格弱耦合非对称储库，（b）半无限一维晶格弱耦合边界储库，（c）无限一维晶格原点附近局部慢键，推导大偏差函数的精确表达式，并通过基于克隆算法的罕见事件模拟进行验证。此外，为半无限SSEP的流大偏差函数提供了初等推导。

Result: 给出了每种构型下粒子流大偏差函数的精确表达式，并通过克隆算法模拟验证了理论结果。同时，为半无限SSEP提供了比现有更简明的初等推导。

Conclusion: 慢键的存在会修改SSEP中粒子流的大偏差行为，本文给出的精确表达式和模拟验证建立了慢键影响的理论框架，初等推导也为半无限情形提供了更易理解的途径。

Abstract: The symmetric simple exclusion process (SSEP) is a paradigmatic model of classical non-equilibrium dynamics. Exact results for large deviations of particle current in the SSEP have been obtained in various settings using integrability-based methods. In this Article, we discuss how these results are modified in the presence of localized slow bonds. We consider three conventional geometries: (a) a finite one-dimensional lattice weakly coupled to unequal reservoirs at its boundaries, (b) a semi-infinite one-dimensional lattice weakly coupled to a boundary reservoir, and (c) an infinite one-dimensional lattice with localized slow bonds near the origin. For each case, we present exact expressions for the large deviation function of current and validate them through rare-event simulations based on the cloning algorithm. In connection with our results, we present an elementary derivation of the exact large deviation function for the current in the semi-infinite SSEP, complementing recent results obtained through more elaborate techniques.

</details>


### [134] [Generalized field mixing for endpoint criticality with marginal flow: resolving the four-state Potts endpoint in the square-lattice $J_1$--$J_2$ Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25946)
*Yihua Sun,Yuchen Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对临界端点（critical endpoint）在存在边缘无关标度场（marginally irrelevant scaling field）时，其渐近临界性被伪一级相变信号掩盖的问题，发展了一套广义场混合（field-mixing）框架。通过构造有限尺寸伪临界流形，该方法去除了相关失谐，暴露出残余边缘漂移，从而实现了基于直方图和Binder比的有限尺寸标度分析。将该方法应用于阻挫方格$J_1$-$J_2$ Ising模型，直接识别出条纹有序端点的位置，并证明其渐近临界性遵循四态Potts普适类并带有对数修正。这一结果解决了该模型长达数十年的争议，并为提取边缘流存在下的端点临界性建立了通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界端点由于伪一级相变信号和边缘无关标度场的对数流，其渐近临界性难以识别，传统的两相关场框架不适用。需要建立新的理论框架来提取这类端点的渐近标度行为。

Method: 1. 发展广义场混合框架，处理一个相关标度场加一个边缘无关标度场的系统；2. 构造有限尺寸伪临界流形，去除相关失谐，暴露残余边缘漂移；3. 结合直方图分析和Binder比进行有限尺寸标度分析；4. 应用于$J_1$-$J_2$ Ising模型，并与Ashkin-Teller模型的Potts点直接比较验证。

Result: 成功将端点作为独立的奇异点直接识别，证明其渐近临界性属于四态Potts普适类并带有对数修正。磁性和向列扇区的Binder标度行为一致，并且与Ashkin-Teller模型的Potts点直接比较得到独立支持。

Conclusion: 解决了阻挫Ising系统中长期存在的数值歧义问题，并为存在边缘流时提取端点渐近临界性建立了通用框架。

Abstract: Identifying the asymptotic criticality of a critical endpoint is challenging, as pseudo-first-order signatures persist over accessible system sizes and mask its underlying critical nature. This ambiguity is amplified at endpoints controlled by a marginally irrelevant scaling field, where logarithmic flow delays the onset of asymptotic scaling. Here we develop a generalized field-mixing framework for endpoint criticality governed by one relevant scaling field together with a marginally irrelevant one, a setting that lies outside the conventional two-relevant-field formulation. By constructing a finite-size pseudocritical manifold, the framework removes the normal relevant detuning and exposes the residual marginal drift, enabling controlled histogram- and Binder-based finite-size analyses. We apply this approach to the frustrated square-lattice $J_1$--$J_2$ Ising model, where the location and even the nature of the stripe-ordering endpoint have remained controversial for decades. The endpoint is isolated directly as a distinct singular point, rather than inferred from where the phase boundary appears most Potts-like, and its asymptotic criticality is shown to follow four-state Potts universality with logarithmic corrections. This identification is independently supported by direct comparison with the Potts point of the Ashkin--Teller model and by consistent Binder scaling in both the magnetic and nematic sectors. Our results resolve a longstanding numerical ambiguity in a paradigmatic frustrated Ising system and establish a general framework for extracting asymptotic endpoint criticality in the presence of marginal flow.

</details>


### [135] [Boltzmann Distribution from Invariance of Coarse-Graining-Scale and Energy-Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25987)
*Weicheng Fu,Yisen Wang,Yong Zhang,Hong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过粗粒化尺度不变性和能量零点平移不变性两个物理条件，简洁推导了经典多体哈密顿系统中单粒子能量分布的玻尔兹曼形式，并通过一维双原子硬核气体和非线性晶格链等模型验证了理论预测与模拟结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种更简洁、物理直观的玻尔兹曼分布推导方法，避免传统统计力学中复杂的系综理论，同时揭示玻尔兹曼因子的本质源于两个基本对称性（尺度不变性和平移不变性）。

Method: 基于粗粒化经验分布的尺度不变性和能量零点的均匀平移不变性，推导出单粒子能量分布须满足函数方程，唯一解为玻尔兹曼因子；参数由平均每粒子能量确定；对可分离哈密顿量，动能部分与位形部分共享同一参数，其中动能部分参数被识别为动力学温度的倒数。

Result: 在一维双原子硬核气体和非线性晶格链模型中，预测的速度、能量、间距、碰撞时间和压力相关位移分布与模拟结果吻合；晶格模型进一步展示了谐波弹性、非谐修正、内压和热膨胀如何从相同的指数平衡权重中涌现。

Conclusion: 该推导方法可推广至任何具有平稳分布和平移不变性的物理量；简要讨论了不同系综之间的关系，为统计力学基础提供了一种更直观的构建路径。

Abstract: We present a concise derivation of the Boltzmann form for single-particle energy distributions in classical many-body Hamiltonian systems. The derivation relies on two physical facts: coarse-graining-scale invariance of the empirical distribution and invariance under a uniform shift of the energy zero. These conditions uniquely yield the Boltzmann factor, whose parameter is fixed by the mean energy per particle. For separable Hamiltonians, the equilibrium weight factorizes into kinetic and configurational contributions sharing the same parameter, identified from the kinetic part as the inverse kinetic temperature. The principle extends to any physical quantity with a stationary distribution and translational invariance. It is illustrated in a one-dimensional diatomic hard-core gas and a nonlinear lattice chain, where it predicts velocity, energy, spacing, collision-time, and pressure-dependent displacement distributions in agreement with simulations. The lattice model further shows how harmonic elasticity, anharmonic corrections, internal pressure, and thermal expansion emerge from the same exponential equilibrium weights. Finally, the relationships among different ensembles are briefly discussed.

</details>
